Fallout New Vegas location scaling reassessment - 2026-10-12
Primary binary interface: radare2 MCP.
Executable: fnv_reverse/FalloutNV.exe, PE32 x86, image base 0x00400000,
FalloutNV 1.4.0.525, SHA-256
42fee7d6cd74e801372aa89c8f71c974cebd3c20ec9ad43d1465b8fa9646b49c.

Interpretation:
- Captures retain exploratory labels, errors and clipped output. Labels are not
  function identities or ABI proof. Native instructions and verified call sites
  take precedence over pdc pseudocode and inferred stack-variable names.
- pdc output is incomplete/orphaned discovery material. The pdg backend could
  not be selected. No pdc pseudocode establishes a contract in the document.
- Large disassemble_function responses are clipped even when a large page_size
  is requested. Bounded disassemble continuations supply the relevant branches;
  this transcript is not complete caller/provider coverage for a patch.
- "light static property dirty producer" is B5BBE0: multibound camera/occluder
  classification, not a lighting dirty producer.
- "graph state reset" is C49850: compound-program jump threading.
- "object map constructor" B63D80 and "accumulator reset map reconstruction"
  B658E0 are destructor-side captures, not proof of per-frame reconstruction.
- "object admission other callers" A59E70 is not a call to C4F320.
- "multibound preparer actual camera position getter" 84E3A0 returns +0x0C;
  the label does not prove camera provenance. The actual classification ABI
  follows the explicit three float stack arguments at verified callers.
- C33C50 starts midinstruction. Only the corrected C33CA8 publication capture
  is used for the plane-cache publication contract.
- C49DC0 starts midinstruction; subsequent aligned jump targets supply the
  bounded compiler tail. The initial bytes are not proof.
- C5AF10 starts inside C5AEF0. Its initial decoded bytes are not proof; this
  graph-registration exploration is not used as a new ownership contract.
- 4472D0 from a header comment was not a valid function start. Its decode is
  rejected and proves no master-index remap contract.
- 542A21 and 55B6E4 are midinstruction discoveries. Separate aligned 542A25
  and 55B6EC captures correct them. XPOD dispatch to 416453, its eight-byte
  read and publication as extra-data type 0x77 establish portal-reference data.
- The native XXXX chunk reader at 472D30 proves replacing the following
  subrecord's 16-bit length with the four-byte extended length.
- Instruction ranges extending past RET include adjacent functions/data;
  only instructions inside the identified function are evidence for it.
- Structural operation budgets are not measured invocation counts, timing,
  runtime provider identity, equivalence of a replacement, or FPS attribution.
